=====CURVE CHECK=====
# Handoff - ENGINEERING_20260627_CURVE_CHECK

Created: 2026-06-27

## Current objective

This harness is for Step06 trajectory/time-fold analytic-curve validation and patch planning. It is not a request to rerun the full project, redesign geometry, rewrite Step06/07/08, or edit manuscript source.

The task is to decide whether the old Step06 analytic curve is physically too coarse, and whether a corrected analytic model can explain the full4 direct prompt / activation-production evidence. The corrected model must separate:

- celestial 511 keV signal atmospheric transmission;
- local prompt-background source-response;
- activation production;
- delayed inventory/activity.

No final delayed selected-rate curve can be promoted or declared wrong without time-specific delayed full replay.

| 页 | 内容 |
|---|---|
| 1–2 | 封面 + 三项工作一句话结论总表 |

| 10–13 | **③ Revan 交叉验证**:DIY veto 设计与质疑 → smoke 三关键数(双序必要性 78.4%、≥1 有效序 100%、不可定序 0.16%)→ 重建层第一遍(对账五位一致、ARM 分离图、Z=8.3–10.0)→ 结论:DIY veto 保留使用 + "结构件皆探测器"教训 |
| 14 | 汇总:头条数字在四种口径下的落点表(3.85 / 4.26 / 3.41–3.97 / 3.0–3.6 ×10⁻⁵,均标注出处)+ 按优先级的下一步清单 |

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Detector-coupled Monte Carlo chain: EXPACS/PARMA atmospheric fields → Geant4/MEGAlib (cosima) transport → Step05 custom event selection (W511=510.58–511.42 keV window, CsI 50 keV veto, Compton topology/FoV veto) → Step06 mission-time fold → Z_20d/F3σ
   - Authority values (fix5_fullstat_v2_exactpos_m50000_s260613): B=0.0392162 cps, S=0.00118587 cps @F0=1e-4, prompt 0.0366410/delayed 0.0025752, Z20d=7.7995, F3σ=3.8464e-5; per-event rate weights: prompt families ≈6.78e-4 Hz/event (γ=5.4278e-3), delayed=0.0025752/30, signal=1.48435e-3/37194
   - Laue optics authority f10m_a1: single Ge(111) ring, f=10 m, 25×18 mm tiles, 81 cm² geometric, Aeff(511)=20.08 cm², mosaic 30″, natural passband 501–521 keV; NEW six-ring multiband (451–551 keV, 1245×2.2 mm tiles) migrated but NOT rate-coupled
   - Physics findings: P1 δ-line artifact (G4 annihilation no Doppler; real source lines 1.3–5.4 keV vs ±0.42 keV window), P2 missing atmospheric 511 line in EXPACS input (grid 449.65→566.08 keV linear interp), P3 GC elevation 27° from 34°N vs 45° window (V404 δ=+33.9° near-zenith consistent)
   - Revan 4.02 batch usage: `-n -a -g GEO -c CFG -C key=val -o out.tra.gz`; critical configs: CoincidenceAlgorithm=0 (replay sims have 1 ns artificial timestamps), UseComptelTypeEvents=true (else 2-site events dropped as a class)
   - Proxy geometry trap: structural volumes (Si substrates, Cu support panels, CsI segments) declared as Scintillator detectors → revan total event energy ≠ spectrometer observable; must use TES-only basis (E_TES from CC HIT truth lines, volumes TP_*)
   - TES detector spec in .det: MDCalorimeter D1–D6, EnergyResolution Gauss σ=0.14 keV, TriggerThreshold 0.3 keV; CsI segments NoiseThreshold=TriggerThreshold=80 keV
   - Two-channel reconstruction sensitivity: ch1 calorimetric (npix≠2) + ch2 npix==2 both-order min-|ARM| vs source axis; Z_comb=√(Z1²+Z2²), T=20d=1.728e6 s
   - Trajectory curve validation: Q(t)=[R_MC(t)/R_MC(REF)]/S_analytic(t); five-layer curve split (signal/prompt_background/activation_production/delayed_inventory/delayed_selected_response); particle-family and family×energy×angle source-response models; leave-one-out pooled fine response
   - TRA/SIM file formats: SE/ET/ID/SQ/CE/CD/CH/PE/PP lines; CC HIT truth comment lines with volume names and edep_keV; IA INIT direction fields (split by ';', dir=parts[-7:-4])
   - matplotlib CJK: font_manager.addfont("/usr/share/fonts/opentype/noto/NotoSansCJK-Regular.ttc") registers as "Noto Sans CJK JP"; unicode super/subscripts must use mathtext

   - `engineering/fullrecon_firstpass_20260702/analyze_fullrecon.py` — key AWK in-stream aggregation:
     ```awk
     function flush() {
         if (npix <= 6) for (v in pix) printf "XP %s %.6f %.6f %.6f %.6f\n", v, pix[v], px[v]/pix[v], py[v]/pix[v], pz[v]/pix[v];
         printf "XS %.6f %.6f %.6f %d\n", etes, ecsi, eoth, npix
     }
     /^CC HIT/ { vol=$3; ... if (vol ~ /^TP_/) { etes += e; ... } else if (vol ~ /^CsI/) { ecsi += e } else { eoth += e } }
     ```
     and both-order ARM: `best_arm_pix(ev, axis)` computes min |ARM| over both pixel orderings using truth energies (θ_kin from cosθ=1−m_ec²(1/Eg−1/E_tot)) and CC energy-weighted centroids; best-θ policy: `eligible = [t for t in scan if scan[t]["B2_events"] >= 5]`

4. Errors and fixes:
   - **Stale ~/.revan.cfg**: revan --save-cfg failed loading old TibetTES geometry → always pass -g explicitly and save a local cfg
   - **Coincidence merging artifact**: first revan run produced only 38 events (37194/~1000) with all "total energy out of limits" — EventList replay has 1 ns artificial timestamps merged by default 1 μs window → `-C CoincidenceAlgorithm=0`
   - **2-site class dropped**: default UseComptelTypeEvents=false rejected 12,406 events → `-C UseComptelTypeEvents=true`
   - **analyze_fullrecon v1 wrong physics basis**: windowing on revan total energy gave delayed 54→1 and eplus ×15 inflation because structural volumes are declared detectors → v2/v3 TES-only truth basis via awk CC aggregation; validated by exact anchor matches (30323; 17663/12660; 54→33; 60; B=0.043545)
   - **Best-θ statistics void**: θ=5° had 0 background events → Z=inf → added B2_events≥5 policy + Poisson band (±1σ, ×2)
   - **Foreground sleep blocked** by harness → used background `until grep -q ALL_DONE ...` loop
   - **Shell CWD drift**: repeated "no such file" failures because cd persisted into subdirs → switched to absolute paths
   - **matplotlib CJK tofu**: glyphs missing from DejaVu → addfont NotoSansCJK ttc (registers as "Noto Sans CJK JP"); "B₂"→"B2", "ph cm⁻² s⁻¹"→mathtext `$^{-2}

92	  <h2>问题与验证设计:解析曲线能否代替逐时刻输运?</h2>
93	  <div class="rule"></div>
94	  <div class="cols">
95	    <div class="col">
96	      <p>论文的 20 天结果是<b>率级折叠</b>:只在第 15 天参考点做完整输运,其余时刻用解析曲线
97	         缩放(高度→大气深度→透过率/瞬发标量/活化标量)。这条曲线若不真,20 天 Z 与 F<sub>3σ</sub> 都失去依据。</p>
98	      <p>验证办法:在轨迹上另取代表点,做<b>直接输运</b>,与解析预测对比归一化比值
99	         Q(t) = [R<sub>MC</sub>(t)/R<sub>MC</sub>(REF)] / S<sub>analytic</sub>(t)。</p>
100	      <table>
101	        <tr><th>点</th><th>day</th><th>高度 km</th><th>R<sub>c</sub> GV</th><th>作用</th></tr>
102	        <tr><td>REF</td><td class="num">15.00</td><td class="num">38.75</td><td class="num">11.00</td><td>锚点</td></tr>

104	        <tr><td>L2</td><td class="num">18.75</td><td class="num">36.25</td><td class="num">11.01</td><td>低点(晚)</td></tr>
105	        <tr><td>H1</td><td class="num">6.25</td><td class="num">41.25</td><td class="num">11.02</td><td>高点</td></tr>
106	      </table>
107	      <p>规模:低统计 smoke 先行(另含 M1 点,判定"偏差或统计不足"),随后升级 full4 加权直接输运:
108	         <b>544 个输运作业,合计 ~1.0×10⁸ 一次粒子</b>,冻结后进入曲线判定。</p>

1	# Corrected Analytic Curve Model
2	
3	## Physical curve split
4	
5	The Step06 analytic curve must be parsed into five physically distinct curves:
6	
7	1. `signal_curve`: celestial 511 keV signal transmission.
8	2. `prompt_background_curve`: local atmospheric prompt source-response.
9	3. `activation_production_curve`: local particle-field isotope production.
10	4. `delayed_inventory_curve`: history-integrated radioactive inventory/activity.
11	5. `delayed_selected_response_curve`: delayed decay activity folded through detector selection response.
12	
13	## Signal curve
14	
15	`signal_rate(t) = F_511 * A_eff * T_atm_511(t) * epsilon_det`.
16	
17	`T_atm_511` directly belongs here only. It is the atmospheric transmission for celestial 511 keV photons from the target direction.
18	
19	## Prompt background curve
20	
21	`R_prompt_j(t) = sum_b Phi_b(t) * K_prompt[j,b]`.
22	
23	`b` may be total source flux, particle family, family+source-energy, or family+source-energy+angle. The current full4 products support total source flux and particle-family response. For W2 prompt only, `IA INIT` ancestry has now been extracted from the existing full4 SIM files, so an energy-angle response matrix can be built. The important boundary is that REF-only W2 response is sparse; it must be used with uncertainty or pooling/regularization, not as a forced exact analytic line.
24	
25	## Activation production curve
26	
27	`P_k(t) = sum_b Phi_b(t) * Y[k,b]`.
28	
29	Activation production uses local source-card flux, not `T_atm_511`. Current full4 products support total production and family/state/volume grouped production response. The new prompt W2 `IA INIT` extraction does not by itself validate source-energy/source-angle activation production response.
30	
31	## Delayed inventory curve
32	
33	`N_k(t+dt)=N_k(t) exp(-lambda_k dt) + P_k(t)/lambda_k * (1-exp(-lambda_k dt))`.
34	
35	Use `expm1` or the `lambda -> 0` limit to avoid cancellation. Activity is `A_k(t)=lambda_k*N_k(t)`.
36	
37	## Delayed selected response curve
38	
39	`R_delayed_j(t)=sum_k A_k(t)*epsilon[j,k]`.
40	
41	This curve is specified but not validated by the current full4 run. Full4 covers prompt direct transport and activation production only; it does not include time-specific delayed selected-rate replay.
42

class=\"kicker\"><b>总览</b>三个假设,三次检验</div><div class=\"pageno\">2 / 15</div></div>\n  <h2>论文头条数字建立在三个外推假设上,这次逐一检验</h2>\n  <div class=\"rule\"></div>\n  <p>当前论文口径:20 天飞行对点源 511 keV 的 3σ 探测阈值为\n     <b>3.85×10⁻⁵ ph cm⁻² s⁻¹</b>(对应 20 天累计显著度 Z = 7.8)。\n     这个数字隐含三个假设,分别对应三项工作:</p>\n  <div class=\"statrow\">\n    <div class=\"stat\"><div class=\"v\">时间外推</div>\n      <div class=\"k\"><b>假设</b>:第 15 天一次完整模拟,可用解析曲线推到全部 20 天。<br>\n      <b>检验结果</b>:旧曲线被 4 个直接模拟点<span style=\"color:var(--red)\">证伪</span>,\n      按物理成分拆层修正后<span style=\"color:var(--green)\">重新通过检验</span>;头条数字复现不变。</div></div>\n    <div class=\"stat\"><div class=\"v\">输入完整性</div>\n      <div class=\"k\"><b>假设</b>:大气本底输入谱是完整的。<br>\n      <b>检验结果</b>:发现输入谱在 511 keV 处<span style=\"color:var(--red)\">缺失大气湮灭线</span>;\n      补入后阈值 3.85 → <span style=\"color:var(--red)\">4.26</span>×10⁻⁵(+10.6%),结论量级不变。</div></div>\n    <div class=\"stat\"><div class=\"v\">事例重建</div>\n      <div class=\"k\"><b>假设</b>:自研的康普顿事例筛选是可靠的,不需要完整方向重建。<br>\n      <b>检验结果</b>:原生工具交叉验证<span style=\"color:var(--green)\">通过</span>;\n      进一步加入方向信息后 Z 反而升到 <span style=\"color:var(--green)\">8.3–10</span>,阈值不劣化。</div></div>\n  </div>\n  <p>三次检验后,阈值数字的活动范围是 <b>3.0 – 4.3 ×10⁻⁵ ph cm⁻² s⁻¹</b>,\n     始终比现有仪器对同类源的上限(1.6×10⁻⁴)好约 4 倍 —— 头条结论在三个方向上都站得住,\n     但各自还有明确列出的未完成项(见各部分末页与最后一页)。</p>\n  <div class=\"src\">engineering/{trajectory_transport_validation_20260626, ENGINEERING_20260627_CURVE_CHECK, paper_review_p1_p2_p3_data_check_20260702, revan_compton_crosscheck_smoke_20260702, fullrecon_firstpass_20260702}</div>\n</section>\n\n<!-- ============ 3. ① 1/5 设计 ============ -->\n<section class=\"slide\">\n  <div class=\"shead\"><div class=\"kicker\"><b>① 轨迹外推曲线</b>1 / 5 · 问题与验证设计</div><div class=\"pageno\">3 / 15</div></div>\n  <h2>只模拟过第 15 天,其余 19 天全靠一条解析曲线</h2>\n  <div class=\"rule\"></div>\n  <div class=\"cols\">\n    <div class=\"col\">\n      <p>完整的粒子输运模拟太贵,整个 20 天任务只在<b>第 15 天参考时刻</b>\n        (浮空高度 38.75 km)做过一次。其余时刻的信号率与本底率,都是用解析曲线\n        把参考时刻的结果按高度、地磁条件缩放出来的。<b>曲线若不真,20 天的显著度和阈值就都失去依据。</b></p>\n      <p>检验办法:在轨迹上另选三个代表时刻,各做一次<b>完整的直接模拟</b>,与曲线预测对比:</p>\n      <div class=\"callout\"><span class=\"f\"><i>Q</i>(<i>t</i>) =\n        [<i>R</i><sub>直接模拟</sub>(<i>t</i>) / <i>R</i><sub>直接模拟</sub>(参考)] ÷ <i>S</i><sub>曲线</sub>(<i>t</i>)</span>\n        <div class=\"fnote\">曲线正确 ⇔ Q ≈ 1(在统计误差内)。这是对曲线的\"盲考\":直接模拟不使用曲线的任何信息。</div></div>\n      <table>\n        <tr><th>验证点</th><th class=\"num\">任务日</th><th class=\"num\">高度 / km</th><th class=\"num\">地磁截止刚度 / GV</th><th>角色</th></tr>\n        <tr><td>REF(参考点)</td><td class=\"num\">15.00</td><td class=\"num\">38.75</td><td class=\"num\">11.00</td><td>曲线的锚点</td></tr>\n        <tr><td>L1(低点·早)</td><td class=\"num\">3.75</td><td class=\"num\">36.25</td><td class=\"num\">10.98</td><td>大气更深</td></tr>\n        <tr><td>L2(低点·晚)</td><td class=\"num\">18.75</td><td class=\"num\">36.25</td><td class=\"num\">11.01</td><td>同高度、不同时刻</td></tr>\n        <tr><td>H1(高点)</td><td class=\"num\">6.25</td><td # 三处修改全部完成:`engineering/report_html_20260702_curve_atm511_revan/report.html`(现 15 页)

## 1. 修复前后的解析曲线公式已加入(新增第 5 页,左右对照)

- **修复前(单标量,红面板)**:信号 `T(t)=e^(−μ·x(t))`;瞬发本底和活化产生共用同一个标量 `s(t) = exp[(x(t)−x_ref)/30 g·cm⁻²]·(11 GV/R_c(t))^0.2`,并标红"复用同一个标量 ← 问题所在"。公式原文核对自任务折叠代码本体,不是凭记忆写的。
- **修复后(五层,绿面板)**:信号 `F₅₁₁·A_eff·T₅₁₁(t)·ε`、瞬发 `Σ_b Φ_b(t)·K_b`、活化产生 `Σ_b Φ_b(t)·Y_k,b`、延迟库存(产生–衰变积分)、延迟计数 `Σ_k A_k(t)·ε_k`(标注"此层尚未直接验证")。底部一句话点破本质:把"乘一个总标量"换成"各成分通量 × 各自响应再求和"。

## 2. Revan 部分重写,四张新直观图代替原来那张看不懂的曲线图

- **第 11 页 · 双次序锥示意图**(核心):左半画出仪器实际记录的东西(两个像素、两份能量、E₁+E₂=511、不知先后),右半画两种次序假设给出的两个反投影锥——一个锥面恰好穿过源方向、另一个指向完全不同的天区,ARM 的定义直接标在图上。看完这一页,后面所有数字都有了着落。
- **第 12 页 · 次序歧义条图**:三根横条——穷举双序 100% 自洽、Revan 单选只有 78.4% 选中更优次序、无法定序仅 0.16%,标题直接给结论"单选会错约 1/5,穷举两序才稳妥"。
- **第 13 页 · 事例分流图**:信号 30,323 和本底 93 个事例各自如何分进"量热通道/方向通道",最后信号 70.7% 的锥指向源、本底 28 个里只剩 5 个——整个重建层逻辑一张图讲完。
- **第 14 页 · 结果对比**:两根柱(信号 70.7% vs 本底 17.9%)+ 显著度对比柱(7.87 → 9.99,误差棒 8.2–12.3,虚线基线),一眼看出"加方向信息不降反升"。

## 3. 排版重做 + 黑话清理

- 新版式:每页统一页眉(章节名 + 页内进度 + 页码)、结论用统计卡片、公式用带行标签的面板、表格改为细横线极简风、圆角浅色提示框区分"结论/警示/待办"三色,仍是纯白底,无动画。
- 内部代号全部从正文清除并做了机器扫描确认(fix5 / W2 / full4 / smoke / ch1-ch2 / Step06 / npix / all-TES 等正文零命中):"W2 窗"→"511 keV 信号窗"、"full4"→"四个直接模拟点"、"ch1/ch2"→"量热通道/方向通道"、"Step06 补丁"→"主分析代码补丁"。代号只保留在两处刻意为之的地方:每页页脚的证据路径(被追问时指路用),和封面上标明"版本号"的数据基准串。

图片全部重新生成并逐张目检(中文字体、标签压线问题已修);HTML 引用的 10 张图片核对齐全;v1 的三张旧图已删除,`gen_figs_v2.py` 与更新后的 README 留档在同目录。

LINE 902 role=user model=None

1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Revan-native Compton reconstruction smoke cross-check vs custom topology veto.
3	
4	Inputs:
5	  - signal_f10m_a1_fix5_comptel.tra.gz  (revan 4.02, CoincidenceAlgorithm=0,
6	    UseComptelTypeEvents=true, otherwise defaults; fix5 geometry)
7	  - focused-signal sim (for per-event INIT beam directions)
8	
9	Custom-chain reference numbers (step05 response summary, W511 science stream):
10	  raw in-window 30323 = single 17663 + multi 12660 (kept 12052, vetoed 608),
11	  selected 29715 (98.0%).
12	"""
13	import gzip, math, json, sys
14	
15	SIM = "/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/runs/step09_focus_fix5_fullstat_v2_exactpos_m50000_s260613/Opticsim_laue_f10m_a1_fix5_fullstat_v2_exactpos_m50000_s260613.inc1.id1.sim.gz"
16	TRA = "signal_f10m_a1_fix5_comptel.tra.gz"
17	ME = 510.999  # keV
18	WLO, WHI = 510.58, 511.42
19	
20	# ---- 1. per-event INIT directions from sim ----
21	init_dir = {}
22	cur_id = None
23	with gzip.open(SIM, "rt") as f:
24	    for line in f:
25	        if line.startswith("ID "):
26	            cur_id = int(line.split()[1])
27	        elif line.startswith("IA INIT") and cur_id is not None:
28	            p = [x.strip() for x in line.split(";")]
29	            dx, dy, dz = float(p[-7]), float(p[-6]), float(p[-5])
30	            init_dir[cur_id] = (dx, dy, dz)
31	
32	# ---- 2. parse tra ----
33	events = []
34	ev = None
35	with gzip.open(TRA, "rt") as f:
36	    for line in f:
37	        if line.startswith("SE"):
38	            if ev: events.append(ev)
39	            ev = {"type": None, "hits": [], "Eg": None, "Ee": None, "PE": None, "SQ": None}
40	        elif ev is None:
41	            continue
42	        elif line.startswith("ET "):
43	            ev["type"] = line.split()[1]
44	        elif line.startswith("ID "):
45	            ev["id"] = int(line.split()[1])
46	        elif line.startswith("SQ "):
47	            ev["SQ"] = int(line.split()[1])
48	        elif line.startswith("CE "):
49	            t = line.split()
50	            ev["Eg"], ev["Ee"] = float(t[1]), float(t[3])
51	        elif line.startswith("PE "):
52	            ev["PE"] = float(line.split()[1])
53	        elif line.startswith("CH "):
54	            t = line.split()
55	            ev["hits"].append((float(t[2]), float(t[3]), float(t[4]), float(t[5])))
56	    if ev: events.append(ev)
57	
58	def norm(v):
59	    m = math.sqrt(sum(x*x for x in v)); return (v[0]/m, v[1]/m, v[2]/m)
60	def ang(a, b):
61	    d = max(-1.0, min(1.0, sum(x*y for x, y in zip(a, b))))
62	    return math.degrees(math.acos(d))
63	def theta_kin(Eg, Etot):
64	    c = 1.0 - ME*(1.0/Eg - 1.0/Etot)
65	    return math.degrees(math.acos(c)) if -1.0 <= c <= 1.0 else None
66	
67	n_ph = sum(1 for e in events if e["type"] == "PH")
68	n_co = sum(1 for e in events if e["type"] == "CO")
69	n_other = len(events) - n_ph - n_co
70	
71	# in-window splits
72	ph_w = [e for e in events if e["type"] == "PH" and WLO <= e["PE"] <= WHI]
73	co_all = [e for e in events if e["type"] == "CO"]
74	co_w = [e for e in co_all if e["Eg"] is not None and WLO <= e["Eg"]+e["Ee"] <= WHI]
75	co_w2 = [e for e in co_w if e["SQ"] == 2]
76	co_w3 = [e for e in co_w if e["SQ"] >= 3]
77	
78	# ---- 3. ARM of revan-chosen order, per-event beam axis ----
79	def arm_of(e, first, second, Efirst):
80	    d = init_dir.get(e["id"])
81	    if d is None: return None
82	    Etot = e["Eg"] + e["Ee"]
83	    Eg_order = Etot - Efirst
84	    tk = theta_kin(Eg_order, Etot)
85	    if tk is None: return None
86	    axis = norm((second[0]-first[0], second[1]-first[1], second[2]-first[2]))
87	    return ang(d, axis) - tk
88	
89	stats = {}
90	for label, sample in (("SQ2", co_w2), ("SQ3plus", co_w3)):
91	    arms, both_ok10, revan_is_min = [], 0, 0
92	    valid_any = 0
93	    for e in sample:
94	        h = e["hits"]
95	        a1 = arm_of(e, h[0], h[1], h[0][3])
96	        if a1 is not None: arms.append(a1)
97	        if label == "SQ2" and len(h) == 2:
98	            a2 = arm_of(e, h[1], h[0], h[1][3])
99	            Etot = e["Eg"]+e["Ee"]
100	            v1 = theta_kin(Etot-h[0][3], Etot) is not None
101	            v2 = theta_kin(Etot-h[1][3], Etot) is not None
102	            if v1 or v2: valid_any += 1
103	            c1 = abs(a1) if a1 is not None else 999
104	            c2 = abs(a2) if a2 is not None else 999
105	            if min(c1, c2) < 10: both_ok10 += 1
106	            if c1 <= c2: revan_is_min += 1
107	    arms_abs = sorted(abs(a) for a in arms)
108	    def frac(t): return sum(1 for a in arms_abs if a < t)/len(arms_abs) if arms_abs else 0
109	    stats[label] = {
110	        "n_inwindow": len(sample),
111	        "n_with_arm": len(arms),
112	        "median_absARM_deg": arms_abs[len(arms_abs)//2] if arms_abs else None,
113	        "frac_absARM_lt_2deg": round(frac(2), 4),
114	        "frac_absARM_lt_5deg": round(frac(5), 4),
115	        "frac_absARM_lt_10deg": round(frac(10), 4),
116	        "frac_absARM_lt_20deg": round(frac(20), 4),
117	    }
118	    if label == "SQ2":
119	        stats[label]["either_order_absARM_lt_10deg"] = round(both_ok10/len(sample), 4) if sample else None
120	        stats[label]["revan_choice_is_minARM_order"] = round(revan_is_min/len(sample), 4) if sample else None

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The session continues work on the TES_511_Balloon project (balloon-borne 511 keV Laue-lens + TES telescope MC simulation). The active deliverable is an HTML slide report at `engineering/report_html_20260702_curve_atm511_revan/report.html` covering three engineering items: ① trajectory analytic-curve validation (falsified then corrected), ② atmospheric 511 keV line correction, ③ Revan cross-validation of the DIY Compton topology veto. Two revision rounds were requested in this session:
   - **Round 1** (completed): "1.在html中也加入修复前后的解析曲线公式 2.revan校验那一部分写清楚点 图看不懂 给一下直观的图 3.html排版请你更用心一点 简约不简单 而且不要用项目内自己约定的一些名词" — add pre/post-fix analytic curve formulas; make Revan section intelligible with intuitive figures; upgrade layout (simple but refined, white background) and purge project-internal jargon from body text.

The existing figures didn't prove these; per-event evidence and proof-figures were required.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - Old Step06 single-scalar curve model (now falsified): `T_atm(t)=exp(−μ_eff·x(t))` (μ_eff anchored so T(x_ref)=T_ref); `prompt_scale s(t)=exp[(x(t)−x_ref)/30 g·cm⁻²]·(11 GV/R_c(t))^0.2` applied to BOTH prompt background and activation production (PROMPT_ATTEN_DEPTH_G_CM2=30.0, exponent 0.2 empirical)
   - Corrected five-layer model: 1) signal `R=F₅₁₁·A_eff·T₅₁₁(t)·ε_det`; 2) prompt `R=Σ_b Φ_b(t)·K_b` (per particle family; window layer needs K_{b,E,θ}); 3) activation production `P_k=Σ_b Φ_b(t)·Y_{k,b}`; 4) delayed inventory `N_k(t+Δt)=N_k e^{−λΔt}+(P_k/λ)(1−e^{−λΔt})`, A_k=λ_k N_k; 5) delayed counting `R=Σ_k A_k(t)·ε_k` (unvalidated)
   - TRA file structure: SE/ET(PH|CO|UN)/ID/SQ/CE/CH(revan hits in chosen order)/PE/BD lines + CC HIT truth comment lines (volume TP_* = TES pixels, edep_keV=, x=, y=, z= tokens); per-event INIT directions joined from sim IA INIT lines (split by ';', dir=parts[-7:-5])
   - ARM convention (matching analyze_smoke.py): axis = norm(second−first hit), ARM = ang(init_dir, axis) − θ_kin; θ_kin from cosθ=1−m_e c²(1/E_γ′−1/E_tot), ME=510.999; order valid iff |cosθ|≤1 (for E_tot=511: first deposit ≤340.67 keV); full-absorption 511 two-site events always have ≥1 valid order (analytic guarantee)
   - Two position bases: basis T = truth energy-weighted centroids (idealized, median |ARM| 5.4°); basis P = revan CH cluster positions = discretized pixel-center readout (instrument-real, median 16.9°); ordering conclusions stable across bases (77.8% vs 77.6% vs original noised 78.4%)
   - Claim-2 by-construction argument: DIY evaluates {order1, order2}; Revan's single choice ∈ that set → min-|ARM| ≤ chosen-|ARM| per event → DIY cumulative acceptance curve ≥ Revan's everywhere
   - Revan config pitfalls: CoincidenceAlgorithm=0 (1 ns artificial timestamps), UseComptelTypeEvents=true (default drops the entire 2-hit-no-track class: 12,406 events in this dataset)
   - Authority numbers: B=0.0392 cps, Z20d=7.80/7.87 (sameT recompute), F3σ=3.85e-5; +atm511 Harris → 4.26e-5 (+10.6%); recon first-pass Z=9.99 (band 8.25–12.3), F3σ=3.0e-5; total background window+veto 0.043545 cps 5-digit match
   - matplotlib CJK: `_fm.fontManager.addfont("/usr/share/fonts/opentype/noto/NotoSansCJK-Regular.ttc")` → "Noto Sans CJK JP"; ✗ (U+2717) missing from font, use ×
   - HTML deck design system: CSS variables (--ink #1c2733, --accent #1f5aa8, --red #b3392f, --green #217a4b, --hair #e4e8ec), .shead header strip, .stat cards, .fpanel/.frow formula panels, .callout good/warn, minimal tables (horizontal rules only), .src evidence footers, keyboard nav JS, print CSS

Key function:
     ```python
     def arm_order(d, first_c, second_c, Efirst, Etot):
         tk = theta_kin(Etot - Efirst, Etot)
         if tk is None: return None
         axis = norm((second_c[0]-first_c[0], ...))
         return abs(ang(d, axis) - tk)
     ```

锚点已定位完毕（只做了 5 个只读文件核对，未执行任何计算或修改）。下面是规划产出：先给我的差距判断，再给审计计划，最后是可直接粘贴给 Codex 的任务说明。

---

## 一、4.639e-5 的出处（已核实，非猜测）

- **4.2624e-5 @20d** = Mass_model_511 full-stat 链 Step08 时变折叠：Z20d=7.038，S=2030.7 counts，B=83 242 counts（`step08_significance/outputs_Mass_model_511_fullstat_v1/…summary.json`）
- **4.639e-5 @20d** = 上值 + Harris 大气 511 线本底 0.008880 cps（`15_p1_p2_p3_replay_20260703/`，`16_completion_audit_20260704/COMPLETION_AUDIT.md`）
- 对照：论文权威 fix5 是 3.846e-5（Z20d=7.80）。两链 B 差 1.19σ，**统计上不可分**——整个本底只有 93 个选后事例（prompt 65 + delayed 28），B 自带 ±11.5% 统计误差。

## 二、差距的数学骨架（规划依据）

F3σ = 3·√(B/T)/A_net，其中 A_net = S/F0 = 11.85 cm²（= 透镜 Aeff 20.08 × 大气透过 0.739 × 选择效率 0.799，三因子对账吻合）。

- 30 天平稳外推：4.639e-5 × √(20/30) ≈ **3.79e-5** → 距 1e-5 还差 **×3.8**。
- 要凑出 ×3.8：A_net×3.8（透镜 20→76 cm²），或 B÷14.4（0.057→0.004 cps），或组合。
- 本底组成（含 Harris）：prompt 77.3% / 大气 511 线 15.6% / 延迟活化 7.0%。**延迟清零只换来 F3×0.964**——活化不是杠杆；大气 511 线从孔径进来，近似不可屏蔽。
- 时变折叠本身几乎是平的（20 天平均 B=0.04817 vs day-15 静态 0.04807，差 0.2%），所以"旧证伪曲线残留"对 20d 数的影响上限很小，但要定量确认。

**初步根因排序（待 Codex 证实/证伪）**：
1. **结构性设计限制**（最可能主因）：20 cm² 透镜 + ~0.05 cps 本底在数学上到不了 1e-5@30d；没有哪个单一 bug 能还回 ×3.8。
2. **分析侧红利未用足**：方向重建通道（穷举双序 ARM）在 fix5 基上实测 Z 7.87→9.99、门槛→3.0e-5，×1.3 增益摆在桌上，只被 ch2 本底 5 事例统计卡住。
3. **本底统计太弱（93 事例）**：既让 B 有 ±11.5% 浮动，也让任何窗口/veto/cut 优化在统计上不可做——这是所有优化工作的前置瓶颈。
4. **诚实性修正可能让差距更大**：MC 511 是 δ 线，真实线宽 ≥1.3 keV vs ±0.42 keV 窗（物理审查估 Z 7.8→6–7）；Harris 线按常数加入、未随深度/Rc 时变。
5. **归一化错误**：历史上高发（×100、×2.22），本链审计标 PASS，先验低，但值得一次独立端到端复算封口。

## 三、交给 Codex 的审计计划（四阶段）

**A. 复算与追源**（每项独立复算，不信任现成 JSON）：① 从 `cumulative_significance_by_case.csv` 原始数据复算 Z20d/F3(20d)（含/不含 Harris）；② A_net 三因子分解对账；③ B 按粒子族分解 + 等效时间账（抽 2 个 tag 从 TT 总和端到端重推）；④ Harris transfer 0.381 cps/(单位通量) 归一化重推 + 与 EXPACS 连续谱无重复计数确认 + 常数近似的时变误差界；⑤ Step06 折叠实际用的公式 vs 已证伪单标量，静态/折叠差定界。

**B. 30d 外推**：给出 F3(30d)，两套假设（末日条件平稳保持 / 活化继续累积），与 naive /√1.5 对比。

**C. 差距预算表**：每个候选根因给"理想化后 F3 变多少"（delayed→0、可屏蔽 prompt 减半/清零、窗口宽度、重建通道 ×1.3、透镜面积需求反解），输出排序 + 证据等级（实测/推导/假设）。

**D. 产出**：`engineering/sensitivity_gap_audit_20260704/`（AUDIT.md + audit_metrics.json + gap_budget.csv），逐检查 PASS/FAIL/FLAG，全部数字带文件路径。

   × T_atm(0.739) × ε_sel(≈0.799)。给出实际乘积与偏差百分比。

A4 Harris 大气511线核查：重推 transfer 归一化（3e6 事例、单位通量定义、立体角/面法向口径）；核对 Harris Rc11–13 GV 线通量取值与 p2_mass_model_511_atm511_flux_scenarios.csv 各情景；确认与 EXPACS 光子连续谱无双重计数（EXPACS 输入在 449.65–566.08 keV 为线性插值、无 511 线——可引 core_md/balloon511_ea_latex_drafts/physics_review_publishability_20260702.md）；评估常数 0.00888 cps 不随深度/Rc 时变近似对 20d 积分的误差方向与量级。
A5 时变折叠残差：确认 step06 (stepwise_maintenance/step06_mission_time_variation/outputs_Mass_model_511_fullstat_v1/ + code/build_v3p5_centerfinger_step06_time_axis.py) 实际使用的缩放公式是否仍为已证伪的单标量 s(t)=exp((x-x_ref)/30)*(11/Rc)^0.2（证伪证据在 ENGINEERING_20260627_CURVE_CHECK/outputs/）。用静态 day-15 B×T vs 折叠后 83242 counts 之差给折叠效应定界，并结合证伪幅度给残差上界。
A6 能窗诚实性：当前 W2 窗 ±0.42 keV，MC 511 是 δ 线；真实线宽+TES 分辨 ≥1.3 keV。引 physics_review_publishability_20260702.md 的 Z 7.8→6–7 粗算，给诚实修正后的当前 F3 区间。
B 30d 外推：用 step06 的 background_time_variation.csv 时轴。若剖面只到 20d，明确写末态保持假设；给两版 F3(30d)：(i) 平稳保持 (ii) 延迟活化按 step06 库存模型继续累积；与 naive 4.639e-5/sqrt(1.5) 对比。

D 结论与产出：写 engineering/sensitivity_gap_audit_20260704/AUDIT.md（含根因排序表+每项证据+置信度）、audit_metrics.json（所有复算数）、gap_budget.csv。最后一节回答三个问题：(1) 4.639e-5@20d 是否成立(或修正为多少)；(2) F3(30d) 最可辩护值与区间；(3) 差距 ×N 的分解：多少来自设计(A_eff/孔径本底)、多少来自未用足的分析、多少来自可疑误差。注意：本底仅 93 事例，所有比较需带统计误差；两链 B 差仅 1.19σ，禁止把统计涨落说成物理差异。"

Execution mode:

- If the request includes `--background`, run the `codex:codex-rescue` subagent in the background.
- If the request includes `--wait`, run the `codex:codex-rescue` subagent in the foreground.
- If neither flag is present, default to foreground.
- `--background` and `--wait` are execution flags for Claude Code. Do not forward them to `task`, and do not treat them as part of the natural-language task text.
- `--model` and `--effort` are runtime-selection flags. Preserve them for the forwarded `task` call, but do not treat them as part of the natural-language task text.
- If the request includes `--resume`, do not ask whether to continue. The user already chose.
- If the request includes `--fresh`, do not ask whether to continue. The user already chose.
- Otherwise, before starting Codex, check for a resumable rescue thread from this Claude session by running:

```bash
node "/home/ubuntu/.claude/plugins/cache/openai-codex/codex/1.0.5/scripts/codex-companion.mjs" task-resume-candidate --json
```

- If that helper reports `available: true`, use `AskUserQuestion` exactly once to ask whether to continue the current Codex thread or start a new one.
- The two choices must be:
  - `Continue current Codex thread`
  - `Start a new Codex thread`
- If the user is clearly giving a follow-up instruction such as "continue", "keep going", "resume", "apply the top fix", or "dig deeper", put `Continue current Codex thread (Recommended)` first.
- Otherwise put `Start a new Codex thread (Recommended)` first.
- If the user chooses continue, add `--resume` before routing to the subagent.
- If the user chooses a new thread, add `--fresh` before routing to the subagent.
- If the helper reports `available: false`, do not ask. Route normally.

Operating rules:

- The subagent is a thin forwarder only. It should use one `Bash` call to invoke `node "/home/ubuntu/.claude/plugins/cache/openai-codex/codex/1.0.5/scripts/codex-companion.mjs" task ...` and return that command's stdout as-is.
- Return the Codex companion stdout verbatim to the user.
- Do not paraphrase, summarize, rewrite, or add commentary before or after it.
- Do not ask the subagent to inspect files, monitor progress, poll `/codex:status`, fetch `/codex:result`, call `/codex:cancel`, summarize output, or do follow-up work of its own.
- Leave `--effort` unset unless the user explicitly asks for a specific reasoning effort.
- Leave the model unset unless the user explicitly asks for one. If they ask for `spark`, map it to `gpt-5.3-codex-spark`.
- Leave `--resume` and `--fresh` in the forwarded request. The subagent handles that routing when it builds the `task` command.
- If the helper reports that Codex is missing or unauthenticated, stop and tell the user to run `/codex:setup`.
- If the user did not supply a request, ask what Codex should investigate or fix.

8	A3 PASS，低统计 caveat。按 [event_catalog.pkl](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/stepwise_maintenance/step05_veto_time_axis/outputs_Mass_model_511_fullstat_v1_l1/work/event_catalog.pkl) 聚合 W2 prompt：`65` final events、`0.0440889481 cps`，与 Step05 一致。tag 分解：`eplus 45 / 0.030529918 cps`，`n 18 / 0.012209929 cps`，`muplus 2 / 0.001349101 cps`，`gamma 0 final`。从 [prompt_normalization_audit.csv](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/stepwise_maintenance/step05_veto_time_axis/outputs_Mass_model_511_fullstat_v1_l1/prompt_normalization_audit.csv) 和 `.dat` TT 独立抽查：gamma `12` files、`TT=184.1818 s`、`0/TT=0 cps`；neutron `8` files、`TT=1474.21 s`、`18/TT=0.012209929 cps`。未见 ×100、×2.22、÷8 复制因子错误。
9	
10	A4 PASS + FLAG。P2 transfer 从 [P2 summary](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/engineering/Mass_model_511_nearfield_migration_20260701/15_p1_p2_p3_replay_20260703/p2_mass_model_511_atm511_transfer_summary.json) 复算：`101 / 265.037 = 0.381078868 cps/(ph cm^-2 s^-1)`；Harris `4.38e-2 * 0.532 = 0.0233016`，加项 `0.008879747 cps`。EXPACS 无 511 线依据 [physics_review](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/core_md/balloon511_ea_latex_drafts/physics_review_publishability_20260702) 的 449.65–566.08 keV 线性插值说明。FLAG：`0.00888 cps` 是无高度/Rc 时变的常数近似，P2 文件也标注 no altitude rescaling。
11	
12	A5 FLAG。Step06 代码仍使用已证伪标量 `exp((depth-depth_ref)/30)*(11/Rc)^0.2`，见 [Step06 code](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/stepwise_maintenance/step06_mission_time_variation/code/build_v3p5_centerfinger_step06_time_axis.py)。但对当前 20d 积分影响小：day-15 静态 `B*T=83071.092` counts vs Step06 fold `83327.876` counts，`+0.309%`，来自 [background_time_variation.csv](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/stepwise_maintenance/step06_mission_time_variation/outputs_Mass_model_511_fullstat_v1/background_time_variation.csv)。方法学仍需 FLAG，因为曲线审计给旧标量 W2 最大残差 `60.7%`、activation `16.6%`，见 [residual_summary.json](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/engineering/ENGINEERING_20260627_CURVE_CHECK/outputs/05_direct_comparison/residual_summary.json)。
13

15	
16	**30d 外推**
17	用 20d profile 后末态保持，Harris included：`F3(30d)=3.776892527e-5`，`Z=7.943037771`。延迟库存继续按 Step06 ODE 累积：`3.776926963e-5`。naive `4.639334e-5/sqrt(1.5)=3.788000469e-5`。来源：[Mass Step08 CSV](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/stepwise_maintenance/step08_significance/outputs_Mass_model_511_fullstat_v1/cumulative_significance_by_case.csv)、[Step06 background CSV](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/stepwise_maintenance/step06_mission_time_variation/outputs_Mass_model_511_fullstat_v1/background_time_variation.csv)、[groundstate CSV](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/runs/Mass_model_511_nearfield_migration_20260701/step02_delay_fix_candidate_Mass_model_511_fullstat_v1/groundstate_activity_corrections.csv)。
18	
19	**Gap Budget**
20	当前 30d baseline `F3=3.77e-5`。理想 delayed→0：`3.64e-5`。非 gamma prompt 减半：`2.95e-5`。非 gamma prompt 清零：`1.79e-5`。方向重建 `1.28–1.4x`：`2.95e-5` 到 `2.70e-5`，但来源 [fullrecon_metrics.json](/home/ubuntu/TES_511_Balloon/engineering/fullrecon_firstpass_20260702/fullrecon_metrics.json) 是 fix5 非权威且 ch2 本底仅 `5` 事例。最乐观组合：prompt other 清零 + recon `1.4x` 得 `1.279e-5`；要到 `1e-5` 还需 `A_net x1.279`，即 `A_net≈15.15 cm²` 或 `Aeff≈25.69 cm²`。
21	
22	最终回答：`4.639e-5@20d` 成立到当前精度；30d 最可辩护值约 `3.78e-5`，现实线宽/窗口区间约 `3.35e-5–3.94e-5`。差距 `×3.8` 主要是设计/本底经济学，不是归一化 bug；可用分析大约贡献 `×1.28–1.4`，可疑误差目前没有证据能解释主差距。
